[31m+ function extractOpenRouterText(data: any) {[39m
[31m+   const d = data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;[39m
[31m+   if (typeof d === "string") return d.trim();[39m
[31m+   if (Array.isArray(d))[39m
[31m+     return d[39m
[31m+       .map((part: any) =>[39m
[31m+         typeof part === "string" ? part : String(part?.text || ""),[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .join("")[39m
[31m+       .trim();[39m
[31m+   return "";[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ async function callOpenRouter(messages: any[], temperature: number) {[39m
[31m+   const key = process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY;[39m
[31m+   if (!key) throw new Error("OPENROUTER_API_KEY bulunamadı.");[39m
[31m+   const freeModel = "openrouter/free";[39m
[31m+   const primaryModel = process.env.OPENROUTER_MODEL?.trim() || freeModel;[39m
[31m+   const requestModel = async (model: string, maxTokens?: number) => {[39m
[31m+     const response = await fetch([39m
[31m+       "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",[39m
[31m+       {[39m
[31m+         method: "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: {[39m
[31m+           Authorization: `Bearer ${key.trim()}`,[39m
[31m+           "Content-Type": "application/json",[39m
[31m+           "X-Title": "NEXUS Kairo Studio",[39m
[31m+         },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           model,[39m
[31m+           messages,[39m
[31m+           temperature,[39m
[31m+           ...(maxTokens ? { max_tokens: maxTokens } : {}),[39m
[31m+         }),[39m
[31m+       },[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));[39m
[31m+     return { response, data };[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+   let { response, data } = await requestModel(primaryModel);[39m
[31m+   const affordableTokens = Number([39m
[31m+     String(data?.error?.message || "").match(/can only afford\s+(\d+)/i)?.[1],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   if (!response.ok && affordableTokens >= 40) {[39m
[31m+     ({ response, data } = await requestModel([39m
[31m+       primaryModel,[39m
[31m+       Math.max(32, affordableTokens - 8),[39m
[31m+     ));[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   if (!response.ok)[39m
[31m+     throw new Error([39m
[31m+       data?.error?.message || `OpenRouter hatası: HTTP ${response.status}`,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   let text = extractOpenRouterText(data);[39m
[31m+   if (!text) {[39m
[31m+     ({ response, data } = await requestModel(primaryModel));[39m
[31m+     if (!response.ok)[39m
[31m+       throw new Error([39m
[31m+         data?.error?.message || `OpenRouter hatası: HTTP ${response.status}`,[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     text = extractOpenRouterText(data);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   if (!text) throw new Error("OpenRouter boş yanıt döndürdü.");[39m
[31m+   return text;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   text: string;[39m

[31m+ async function generateTextResult([39m
[31m+   system: string,[39m
[31m+   messages: any[],[39m
[31m+   temperature: number,[39m
[31m+   preferredProvider: string,[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<GeneratedTextResult> {[39m
[31m+   const hasOpenRouter = Boolean(process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY?.trim());[39m
[31m+   const hasGemini = Boolean(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim());[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (preferredProvider === "openrouter" && hasOpenRouter) {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const text = await callOpenRouter([39m
[31m+         [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],[39m
[31m+         temperature,[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return { text, providerUsed: "openrouter" };[39m
[31m+     } catch (openRouterErr) {[39m
[31m+       console.warn("[Provider] OpenRouter failed, falling back to Gemini:", openRouterErr);[39m
[31m+       if (hasGemini) {[39m
[31m+         const response = await getGeminiClient().models.generateContent({[39m
[31m+           model: "gemini-3.6-flash",[39m
[31m+           contents: messages.map((m) => ({[39m
[31m+             role: m.role === "assistant" ? "model" : "user",[39m
[31m+             parts: [{ text: m.content }],[39m
[31m+           })),[39m
[31m+           config: { systemInstruction: system },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         return { text: (response?.text || "").trim(), providerUsed: "gemini" };[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       throw openRouterErr;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (hasGemini) {[39m
[31m+     const response = await getGeminiClient().models.generateContent({[39m
[31m+       model: "gemini-3.6-flash",[39m
[31m+       contents: messages.map((m) => ({[39m
[31m+         role: m.role === "assistant" ? "model" : "user",[39m
[31m+         parts: [{ text: m.content }],[39m
[31m+       })),[39m
[31m+       config: { systemInstruction: system },[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return { text: (response?.text || "").trim(), providerUsed: "gemini" };[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (hasOpenRouter) {[39m
[31m+     const text = await callOpenRouter([39m
[31m+       [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],[39m
[31m+       temperature,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     return { text, providerUsed: "openrouter" };[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   throw new Error("Yapay zeka anahtarı (GEMINI_API_KEY veya OPENROUTER_API_KEY) bulunamadı.");[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ async function generateText([39m
[31m+   system: string,[39m
[31m+   messages: any[],[39m
[31m+   temperature: number,[39m
[31m+   preferredProvider: string,[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<string> {[39m

[31m+ async function getFastRecentMemory(userId: string, kairaInstanceId: string) {[39m
[31m+   const cacheKey = memoryCacheKey(userId, kairaInstanceId);[39m
[31m+   const c = memoryCache.get(cacheKey);[39m
[31m+   if (c && c.expires > Date.now()) return c.items;[39m
[31m+   const scopedUserId = stateOwnerScope(userId, kairaInstanceId);[39m
[31m+   const loader = loadRecentKdmMemory(FAST_RECENT_MEMORY_LIMIT, scopedUserId)[39m
[31m+     .then([39m
[31m+       (items) => ([39m
[31m+         memoryCache.set(cacheKey, { expires: Date.now() + MEMORY_TTL_MS, items }),[39m
[31m+         items[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     .catch(() => []);[39m
[31m+   return Promise.race([loader, sleep(700, [])]);[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function sessionTokens(text: string) {[39m

[31m+     ),[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+   if (!Array.isArray(history) || history.length <= 8)[39m
[31m+     return "Yakın oturum geçmişi yeterli; ek çalışma hafızası yok.";[39m
[31m+   const older = history.slice(0, -8),[39m
[31m+     tokens = sessionTokens(userMessage);[39m
[31m+   const scored = older[39m
[31m+     .map((item: any, index: number) => {[39m
[31m+       const text = String(item?.text || "");[39m
[31m+       const low = text.toLocaleLowerCase("tr-TR");[39m
[31m+       const overlap = tokens.reduce((n, t) => n + (low.includes(t) ? 1 : 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       const recency = (index + 1) / Math.max(1, older.length);[39m
[31m+       return { item, index, score: overlap * 10 + recency };[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+     .filter((x) => x.score >= 10)[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)[39m
[31m+     .slice(0, 6)[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);[39m
[31m+   if (!scored.length)[39m
[31m+     return "Bu soruyla ilgili daha eski aynı-oturum kaydı bulunamadı.";[39m
[31m+   return scored[39m
[31m+     .map([39m
[31m+       (x) =>[39m
[31m+         `${x.item.sender === "user" ? x.item.participantName || "Kullanıcı" : "Kaira"}: ${String(x.item.text || "").slice(0, 320)}`,[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     .join("\n");[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ const defaultDynamicState: DroitDynamicState = {[39m
[31m+   calmness: 70,[39m

[31m+   stress: 20,[39m

[31m+ function normalizeKdmSemanticAliases(message: string) {[39m
[31m+   return message[39m
[31m+     .replace(/\bmalsın\b/gi, "salaksın")[39m
[31m+     .replace(/\bmal\b/gi, "salak");[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function buildEntityGroundingInstruction(entityResolution: any) {[39m
[31m+   if (!entityResolution) return "";[39m
[31m+   const refs = Array.isArray(entityResolution.references)[39m
[31m+     ? entityResolution.references[39m
[31m+         .map((ref: any) => {[39m
[31m+           const resolved = ref.resolvedName || ref.resolvedId || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+           return `${ref.surface} => ${resolved} (rol=${ref.role}, güven=${Number(ref.confidence ?? 0).toFixed(2)})`;[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+         .join("; ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   const ambiguities = Array.isArray(entityResolution.ambiguities) && entityResolution.ambiguities.length[39m
[31m+     ? entityResolution.ambiguities.join(" | ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   return `ENTITY / WORLD GROUNDING:\nKonuşan kişi: ${entityResolution.speaker?.name || "bilinmiyor"}.\nMuhatap: ${entityResolution.addressee?.name || "Kaira"}.\nReferanslar: ${refs}.\nBelirsizlikler: ${ambiguities}.\nKURALLAR: Birinci şahıs (ben/bana/beni) konuşan kişiye, ikinci şahıs (sen/sana/seni) Kaira'ya aittir. Açık isim çözümü mevcut konuşanla aynı kişiye çıkıyorsa bunu otomatik olarak ayrı bir üçüncü şahıs yapma. Belirsizlik varsa kişi/olay ataması UYDURMA; cevabı belirsizliği koruyacak şekilde yaz. Kullanıcının söylemediği "bunu ben söyledim", "şu kişi yaptı" gibi yeni bir kaynak/aktör icat etme.`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function buildWorldEventInstruction(worldEvent: any) {[39m
[31m+   if (!worldEvent) return "";[39m
[31m+   const actor = worldEvent.actor?.name || worldEvent.actor?.id || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+   const target = worldEvent.target?.name || worldEvent.target?.id || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+   const ambiguities = Array.isArray(worldEvent.ambiguities) && worldEvent.ambiguities.length[39m
[31m+     ? worldEvent.ambiguities.join(" | ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   return `CANONICAL WORLD EVENT:\nOlay tipi: ${worldEvent.eventType || "unknown"}.\nActor: ${actor}.\nTarget: ${target}.\nAktarılan söz: ${worldEvent.reportedSpeech ? "evet" : "hayır"}.\nKesinlik: ${Number(worldEvent.certainty ?? 0).toFixed(2)}.\nBelirsizlikler: ${ambiguities}.\nKURAL: Bu olay haritasını kaynak gerçekliği olarak kullan. Actor veya target çözülmemişse kimlik UYDURMA. Kullanıcının söylemediği yeni bir fail, hedef veya olay ekleme.`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ app.post("/api/chat", async (req, res) => {[39m
[31m+   const serverStart = now();[39m
[31m+   let idempotencyKey = "";[39m
[31m+   let ownsIdempotencyClaim = false;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const {[39m
[31m+       userId = "anonymous",[39m
[31m+       userName = "Kullanıcı",[39m
[31m+       userMessage,[39m
[31m+       character = {},[39m
[31m+       personality,[39m
[31m+       responsePersonality: incomingResponsePersonality,[39m
[31m+       history = [],[39m
[31m+       dynamicState = defaultDynamicState,[39m
[31m+       provider = "openrouter",[39m
[31m+       suppressRecentMemory = false,[39m

[31m+       requestId: incomingRequestId,[39m

[31m+     if (!userMessage)[39m
[31m+       return res.status(400).json({ error: "userMessage is required" });[39m
[31m+     const kairaInstance = resolveKairaInstanceContext({[39m
[31m+       instanceId: incomingKairaInstanceId,[39m
[31m+       instanceType: incomingKairaInstanceType as KairaInstanceType | undefined,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     const kairaPolicy = instancePolicy(kairaInstance.instanceType);[39m
[31m+     const safeUserId = String(userId).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_");[39m
[31m+     const stateUserId = stateOwnerScope(userId, kairaInstance.instanceId);[39m
[31m+     const sessionId = incomingSessionId?.trim() || `session_${stateUserId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_")}`;[39m
[31m+     const requestId = typeof incomingRequestId === "string" ? incomingRequestId.trim().slice(0, 160) : "";[39m
[31m+     idempotencyKey = requestId ? `${stateUserId}::${kairaInstance.instanceId}::${requestId}` : "";[39m
[31m+     if (idempotencyKey) {[39m
[31m+       const claim = await claimCoordinatedKairaChatRequest<any>(idempotencyKey);[39m
[31m+       if (claim.kind === "replay") return res.json(claim.payload);[39m
[31m+       if (claim.kind === "wait") {[39m
[31m+         const outcome = await claim.outcome;[39m
[31m+         if (outcome.ok === true) return res.json(outcome.payload);[39m
[31m+         throw new Error(outcome.errorMessage);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       ownsIdempotencyClaim = true;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const sendChatPayload = async (payload: any) => {[39m
[31m+       if (idempotencyKey && ownsIdempotencyClaim) {[39m
[31m+         await completeCoordinatedKairaChatRequest(idempotencyKey, payload);[39m
[31m+         ownsIdempotencyClaim = false;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       return res.json(payload);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const activityPermissionResolution = kairaPolicy.autonomousActivityPlanning[39m
[31m+       ? await resolveKairaActivityPermissionChatReply({[39m
[31m+           ownerUserId: userId,[39m
[31m+           kairaInstanceId: kairaInstance.instanceId,[39m
[31m+           sessionId,[39m
[31m+           permissionRequestId:[39m
[31m+             typeof incomingActivityPermissionRequestId === "string"[39m
[31m+               ? incomingActivityPermissionRequestId.trim()[39m
[31m+               : undefined,[39m
[31m+           message: String(userMessage),[39m
[31m+           now: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       : ({ status: "none" } as const);[39m
[31m+     let activityPermissionPrompt: KairaActivityPermissionChatPrompt | null = null;[39m

[31m+       if (!kairaPolicy.autonomousActivityPlanning) return baseReply;[39m

[31m+     const knowledgeProfile =[39m

[31m+     const discourseState = deriveDiscourseState(cleanHistory, {[39m

[31m+ async function callOpenRouter(messages: any[], temperature: number) {[39m
[31m+   const key = process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY;[39m
[31m+   if (!key) throw new Error("OPENROUTER_API_KEY bulunamadı.");[39m
[31m+   const freeModel = "openrouter/free";[39m
[31m+   const primaryModel = process.env.OPENROUTER_MODEL?.trim() || freeModel;[39m
[31m+   const requestModel = async (model: string, maxTokens?: number) => {[39m
[31m+     const response = await fetch([39m
[31m+       "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",[39m
[31m+       {[39m
[31m+         method: "POST",[39m
[31m+         headers: {[39m
[31m+           Authorization: `Bearer ${key.trim()}`,[39m
[31m+           "Content-Type": "application/json",[39m
[31m+           "X-Title": "NEXUS Kairo Studio",[39m
[31m+         },[39m
[31m+         body: JSON.stringify({[39m
[31m+           model,[39m
[31m+           messages,[39m
[31m+           temperature,[39m
[31m+           ...(maxTokens ? { max_tokens: maxTokens } : {}),[39m
[31m+         }),[39m
[31m+       },[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));[39m
[31m+     return { response, data };[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+   let { response, data } = await requestModel(primaryModel);[39m
[31m+   const affordableTokens = Number([39m
[31m+     String(data?.error?.message || "").match(/can only afford\s+(\d+)/i)?.[1],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   if (!response.ok && affordableTokens >= 40) {[39m
[31m+     ({ response, data } = await requestModel([39m
[31m+       primaryModel,[39m
[31m+       Math.max(32, affordableTokens - 8),[39m
[31m+     ));[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   if (!response.ok)[39m
[31m+     throw new Error([39m
[31m+       data?.error?.message || `OpenRouter hatası: HTTP ${response.status}`,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   let text = extractOpenRouterText(data);[39m
[31m+   if (!text) {[39m
[31m+     ({ response, data } = await requestModel(primaryModel));[39m
[31m+     if (!response.ok)[39m
[31m+       throw new Error([39m
[31m+         data?.error?.message || `OpenRouter hatası: HTTP ${response.status}`,[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     text = extractOpenRouterText(data);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   if (!text) throw new Error("OpenRouter boş yanıt döndürdü.");[39m
[31m+   return text;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ async function generateTextResult([39m
[31m+   system: string,[39m

[31m+   preferredProvider: string,[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<GeneratedTextResult> {[39m
[31m+   const hasOpenRouter = Boolean(process.env.OPENROUTER_API_KEY?.trim());[39m
[31m+   const hasGemini = Boolean(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim());[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (preferredProvider === "openrouter" && hasOpenRouter) {[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const text = await callOpenRouter([39m
[31m+         [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],[39m
[31m+         temperature,[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+       return { text, providerUsed: "openrouter" };[39m
[31m+     } catch (openRouterErr) {[39m

[31m+       if (hasGemini) {[39m
[31m+         const response = await getGeminiClient().models.generateContent({[39m
[31m+           model: "gemini-3.6-flash",[39m
[31m+           contents: messages.map((m) => ({[39m
[31m+             role: m.role === "assistant" ? "model" : "user",[39m
[31m+             parts: [{ text: m.content }],[39m
[31m+           })),[39m
[31m+           config: { systemInstruction: system },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         return { text: (response?.text || "").trim(), providerUsed: "gemini" };[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       throw openRouterErr;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (hasGemini) {[39m
[31m+     const response = await getGeminiClient().models.generateContent({[39m
[31m+       model: "gemini-3.6-flash",[39m
[31m+       contents: messages.map((m) => ({[39m
[31m+         role: m.role === "assistant" ? "model" : "user",[39m
[31m+         parts: [{ text: m.content }],[39m
[31m+       })),[39m
[31m+       config: { systemInstruction: system },[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return { text: (response?.text || "").trim(), providerUsed: "gemini" };[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (hasOpenRouter) {[39m
[31m+     const text = await callOpenRouter([39m
[31m+       [{ role: "system", content: system }, ...messages],[39m
[31m+       temperature,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     return { text, providerUsed: "openrouter" };[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   throw new Error("Yapay zeka anahtarı (GEMINI_API_KEY veya OPENROUTER_API_KEY) bulunamadı.");[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ async function getFastRecentMemory(userId: string, kairaInstanceId: string) {[39m

[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     .catch(() => []);[39m

[31m+ function sessionTokens(text: string) {[39m
[31m+   return Array.from([39m
[31m+     new Set([39m
[31m+       text[39m

[31m+     ),[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ function buildSessionWorkingMemory(history: any[], userMessage: string) {[39m

[31m+       const text = String(item?.text || "");[39m
[31m+       const low = text.toLocaleLowerCase("tr-TR");[39m
[31m+       const overlap = tokens.reduce((n, t) => n + (low.includes(t) ? 1 : 0), 0);[39m
[31m+       const recency = (index + 1) / Math.max(1, older.length);[39m
[31m+       return { item, index, score: overlap * 10 + recency };[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+     .filter((x) => x.score >= 10)[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)[39m
[31m+     .slice(0, 6)[39m
[31m+     .sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);[39m
[31m+   if (!scored.length)[39m
[31m+     return "Bu soruyla ilgili daha eski aynı-oturum kaydı bulunamadı.";[39m
[31m+   return scored[39m
[31m+     .map([39m
[31m+       (x) =>[39m
[31m+         `${x.item.sender === "user" ? x.item.participantName || "Kullanıcı" : "Kaira"}: ${String(x.item.text || "").slice(0, 320)}`,[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     .join("\n");[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ const defaultDynamicState: DroitDynamicState = {[39m
[31m+   calmness: 70,[39m

[31m+   stress: 20,[39m

[31m+ function normalizeKdmSemanticAliases(message: string) {[39m
[31m+   return message[39m
[31m+     .replace(/\bmalsın\b/gi, "salaksın")[39m
[31m+     .replace(/\bmal\b/gi, "salak");[39m

[31m+ function buildEntityGroundingInstruction(entityResolution: any) {[39m
[31m+   if (!entityResolution) return "";[39m
[31m+   const refs = Array.isArray(entityResolution.references)[39m
[31m+     ? entityResolution.references[39m
[31m+         .map((ref: any) => {[39m
[31m+           const resolved = ref.resolvedName || ref.resolvedId || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+           return `${ref.surface} => ${resolved} (rol=${ref.role}, güven=${Number(ref.confidence ?? 0).toFixed(2)})`;[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+         .join("; ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   const ambiguities = Array.isArray(entityResolution.ambiguities) && entityResolution.ambiguities.length[39m
[31m+     ? entityResolution.ambiguities.join(" | ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   return `ENTITY / WORLD GROUNDING:\nKonuşan kişi: ${entityResolution.speaker?.name || "bilinmiyor"}.\nMuhatap: ${entityResolution.addressee?.name || "Kaira"}.\nReferanslar: ${refs}.\nBelirsizlikler: ${ambiguities}.\nKURALLAR: Birinci şahıs (ben/bana/beni) konuşan kişiye, ikinci şahıs (sen/sana/seni) Kaira'ya aittir. Açık isim çözümü mevcut konuşanla aynı kişiye çıkıyorsa bunu otomatik olarak ayrı bir üçüncü şahıs yapma. Belirsizlik varsa kişi/olay ataması UYDURMA; cevabı belirsizliği koruyacak şekilde yaz. Kullanıcının söylemediği "bunu ben söyledim", "şu kişi yaptı" gibi yeni bir kaynak/aktör icat etme.`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function buildWorldEventInstruction(worldEvent: any) {[39m
[31m+   if (!worldEvent) return "";[39m
[31m+   const actor = worldEvent.actor?.name || worldEvent.actor?.id || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+   const target = worldEvent.target?.name || worldEvent.target?.id || "çözülmedi";[39m
[31m+   const ambiguities = Array.isArray(worldEvent.ambiguities) && worldEvent.ambiguities.length[39m
[31m+     ? worldEvent.ambiguities.join(" | ")[39m
[31m+     : "yok";[39m
[31m+   return `CANONICAL WORLD EVENT:\nOlay tipi: ${worldEvent.eventType || "unknown"}.\nActor: ${actor}.\nTarget: ${target}.\nAktarılan söz: ${worldEvent.reportedSpeech ? "evet" : "hayır"}.\nKesinlik: ${Number(worldEvent.certainty ?? 0).toFixed(2)}.\nBelirsizlikler: ${ambiguities}.\nKURAL: Bu olay haritasını kaynak gerçekliği olarak kullan. Actor veya target çözülmemişse kimlik UYDURMA. Kullanıcının söylemediği yeni bir fail, hedef veya olay ekleme.`;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ app.post("/api/chat", async (req, res) => {[39m
[31m+   const serverStart = now();[39m
[31m+   let idempotencyKey = "";[39m
[31m+   let ownsIdempotencyClaim = false;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const {[39m
[31m+       userId = "anonymous",[39m
[31m+       userName = "Kullanıcı",[39m
[31m+       userMessage,[39m
[31m+       character = {},[39m
[31m+       personality,[39m
[31m+       responsePersonality: incomingResponsePersonality,[39m
[31m+       history = [],[39m

[31m+       provider = "openrouter",[39m

[31m+       sessionId: incomingSessionId,[39m
[31m+       kairaInstanceId: incomingKairaInstanceId,[39m
[31m+       kairaInstanceType: incomingKairaInstanceType,[39m
[31m+       requestId: incomingRequestId,[39m
[31m+       activityPermissionRequestId: incomingActivityPermissionRequestId,[39m
[31m+     } = req.body;[39m
[31m+     if (!userMessage)[39m
[31m+       return res.status(400).json({ error: "userMessage is required" });[39m
[31m+     const kairaInstance = resolveKairaInstanceContext({[39m
[31m+       instanceId: incomingKairaInstanceId,[39m
[31m+       instanceType: incomingKairaInstanceType as KairaInstanceType | undefined,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     const kairaPolicy = instancePolicy(kairaInstance.instanceType);[39m
[31m+     const safeUserId = String(userId).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_");[39m
[31m+     const stateUserId = stateOwnerScope(userId, kairaInstance.instanceId);[39m
[31m+     const sessionId = incomingSessionId?.trim() || `session_${stateUserId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_")}`;[39m
[31m+     const requestId = typeof incomingRequestId === "string" ? incomingRequestId.trim().slice(0, 160) : "";[39m
[31m+     idempotencyKey = requestId ? `${stateUserId}::${kairaInstance.instanceId}::${requestId}` : "";[39m
[31m+     if (idempotencyKey) {[39m
[31m+       const claim = await claimCoordinatedKairaChatRequest<any>(idempotencyKey);[39m
[31m+       if (claim.kind === "replay") return res.json(claim.payload);[39m
[31m+       if (claim.kind === "wait") {[39m
[31m+         const outcome = await claim.outcome;[39m
[31m+         if (outcome.ok === true) return res.json(outcome.payload);[39m
[31m+         throw new Error(outcome.errorMessage);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       ownsIdempotencyClaim = true;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const sendChatPayload = async (payload: any) => {[39m
[31m+       if (idempotencyKey && ownsIdempotencyClaim) {[39m
[31m+         await completeCoordinatedKairaChatRequest(idempotencyKey, payload);[39m
[31m+         ownsIdempotencyClaim = false;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       return res.json(payload);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const activityPermissionResolution = kairaPolicy.autonomousActivityPlanning[39m
[31m+       ? await resolveKairaActivityPermissionChatReply({[39m
[31m+           ownerUserId: userId,[39m
[31m+           kairaInstanceId: kairaInstance.instanceId,[39m
[31m+           sessionId,[39m
[31m+           permissionRequestId:[39m
[31m+             typeof incomingActivityPermissionRequestId === "string"[39m
[31m+               ? incomingActivityPermissionRequestId.trim()[39m
[31m+               : undefined,[39m
[31m+           message: String(userMessage),[39m
[31m+           now: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       : ({ status: "none" } as const);[39m
[31m+     let activityPermissionPrompt: KairaActivityPermissionChatPrompt | null = null;[39m
[31m+     const attachActivityPermission = async (baseReply: string) => {[39m
[31m+       if (!kairaPolicy.autonomousActivityPlanning) return baseReply;[39m
[31m+       activityPermissionPrompt = await presentKairaActivityPermissionChatPrompt({[39m

[31m+         kairaInstanceId: kairaInstance.instanceId,[39m

[31m+         promptTurnId: `permission_prompt_${requestId || `${sessionId}_${cleanHistory.length}`}`,[39m

[31m+         ? canonicalSemantic.event.knowledgeQuery[39m

[31m+     const knowledgeProfile =[39m

[31m+     const discourseState = deriveDiscourseState(cleanHistory, {[39m
[31m+       message: userMessage,[39m
[31m+       event: languageUnderstanding.event,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     const dialogueDecision = planDialogueResponse([39m

[31m+       userName,[39m
[31m+       languageUnderstanding.event,[39m

[31m+     const memoryStart = now();[39m

[31m+         ? Promise.resolve([])[39m

[31m+           : loadRecentKdmMemory([39m
[31m+               persistentMemoryFetchLimitForDialogueMove(dialogueDecision.move),[39m
[31m+               stateUserId,[39m
